So Rizqun Kareem, this provision that comes regularly again and again in Dhafna, Rizqun and also the word Kareem, the adjective is also in Dhafna, it's huge, it's amazing. all of this is going to come to them, they're going to have this and it's going to be the very best and it's going to be perpetual, constant, should they wish but it will be perpetual and this is their reward this is what Allah SWT says, so that's the thing be believers, be firm believers and be firm in your belief and your confidence in Allah SWT and Allah will give these firm rewards, these amazing rewards words. Okay, let's stop here, and we'll continue from this point, insha'Allah. Wa sallallahu ala sayyidina Muhammadin wa ala alihi wa sahbihi wa sallam walhamdulillahi rabbil alameen. Allah bless you all.

But, you know, this was the obligation. وَإِنْ إِسْتَنْصُرُوكُمْ فِي الدِّينِ فَعَلَيْكُمُ النَّصُرُ But if they seek help from you against persecution in faith, it's your obligation to help them. Because some were still remaining in Makkah either secretly or they were known to be Muslims but they weren't getting that much grief. Then he said, but if they ask you for help, you have to help them.

**[32:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PWjmt2ihYEM&t=1924s)** إِلَّا عَلَىٰ قَوْمٍ بَيْنَكُمْ وَبَيْنَهُمْ مِيثَاقٌ A mithaq is a very, very strong pledge that you emphasize so much that the other person feels confident you won't break it. So he says, unless there's this really strong pledge between you and a people, you have a treaty and alliance with a particular people, and in that case you can't break that. You maintain that alliance and you say to these people, we can't help you. We have a treaty, Allah has told us to stick to our treaties and pledges وَاللَّهُ بِمَا تَعْمَلُونَ بَصِيرًا And Allah, regarding all that you're doing, He sees it all بَصِير, He's got deep knowledge and deep insight as well as sight So He sees everything But usually the word بَصِير is used for someone who's got not just full understanding but also an insight and a deeper understanding of the mechanics and the wisdom behind certain things.

**[33:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PWjmt2ihYEM&t=1985s)** He sees everything, whether you're going to be true, whether you're going to break your pledges, your alliances, whether you're going to help them if it's possible. Allah knows all of that. So stick to what you've agreed. And, you know, Alhamdulillah. So there's just a few short verses left and we'll conclude this surah, inshallah ta'ala, next time. And then we will move on to Surah At-Tawbah InshaAllah Wa Salallahu Ala Sayyidina Muhammadin Wa Ala Alihi Wa Sahbihi Wa Salam Walhamdulillahi Rabbil Alameen Allah bless you all Assalamualaikum
